- Active motifs: wind sent into an object (quranic:root_001529:B001/m01); blowing horn (quranic:root_000891:B004/m01); grave enclosure (quranic:root_000226:B001/m01); stirring a sleeper (quranic:root_000129:B001/m01); collective surge (quranic:root_000129:B004/m01); sleep and stillness (quranic:root_000585:B001/m01); fixed residence (quranic:root_000585:B003/m01); accelerated rejection of sleep (quranic:root_000585:B004/m01); rapid movement (quranic:root_001499:B002/m01); separation and emergence (quranic:root_001499:B003/m01); appointed time or place (quranic:root_001662:B003/m01); fulfilled promise (quranic:root_000852:B004/m01); carried messenger-word (quranic:root_000563:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:51 `نفخ`, `الصور`, `الأجداث`, `ينسلون`; 36:52 `بعثنا`, `مرقدنا`, `وعد`, `صدق`, `المرسلون`
- Synthesis: Breath activates an instrument; the instrument activates bodies. Grave and sleep supply enclosure and stillness, while stirring, accelerated waking, emergence, and rapid movement form the release sequence. The appointment and messenger-word show that this sudden motion was already temporally routed.

#### Subchannel B. One signal gathers all
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A single alarming sound aligns dispersed persons in one direction, collects them, and makes them a present body.
- Active motifs: loud call (quranic:root_000895:B001/m01); alarming cry (quranic:root_000895:B002/m01); numerical one (quranic:root_001631:B002/m01); unification in one direction (quranic:root_001631:B005/m01); collection of dispersed members (quranic:root_000259:B001/m01); gathered crowd (quranic:root_000259:B002/m01); witnessed proximity (quranic:root_000333:B001/m01); present collective (quranic:root_000333:B007/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:53 `صيحة`, `واحدة`, `جميع`, `محضرون`
- Synthesis: Numerical singularity produces social totality. One signal supplies a common vector; gathering then converts dispersed persons into a collective whose defining state is immediate presence.

### 12. P4 - Embodied Accounting
- Semantic invariant: Conduct remains attached to the actor and can be converted into exact compensation and bodily testimony.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:54 states non-displacement and recompense for work, while 36:65 seals mouths and makes hands and feet testify to earnings.
- Surprising reach: A word can also be a wound, so the body's speaking limbs resemble inscribed marks left by action.

#### Subchannel A. Exact settlement of action
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A decisive event-day fixes each self to its act and settles the account without moving a right from its proper holder.
- Active motifs: severe event-day (quranic:root_001700:B003/m01); wrongful displacement (quranic:root_000967:B004/m01); withholding a right (quranic:root_000967:B008/m01); self as the thing itself (quranic:root_001533:B012/m01); reciprocal compensation (quranic:root_000244:B001/m01); one thing standing for another (quranic:root_000244:B002/m01); debt settlement (quranic:root_000244:B003/m01); intentional act (quranic:root_001046:B001/m01); acquired earning (quranic:root_001296:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:54 `اليوم`, `تظلم`, `نفس`, `تجزون`, `تعملون`; 36:65 `يكسبون`
- Synthesis: Justice is modeled as correct placement and settlement. No right is shifted out of position; act, actor, and compensating return remain aligned as in an account whose debt is exactly discharged.

#### Subchannel B. Sealed mouth, evidentiary limbs
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The normal speech aperture is sealed, while hands and feet become witnesses whose marks and earnings speak from the body.
- Active motifs: seal-imprint (quranic:root_000393:B002/m01); speech-preventing seal (quranic:root_000393:B003/m01); open mouth (quranic:root_001190:B001/m01); mouth-produced speech (quranic:root_001190:B003/m01); intelligible exchange (quranic:root_001316:B001/m01); bodily wound as a word-mark (quranic:root_001316:B003/m01); hand as limb (quranic:root_001693:B001/m01); deed attributed to hands (quranic:root_001693:B009/m01); witnessed presence (quranic:root_000822:B001/m01); informed testimony (quranic:root_000822:B002/m01); tongue as witness (quranic:root_000822:B005/m01); evidentiary mark (quranic:root_000822:B008/m01); foot as limb (quranic:root_000546:B001/m01); earning limb (quranic:root_001296:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:65 `نختم`, `أفواههم`, `تكلمنا`, `أيديهم`, `تشهد`, `أرجلهم`, `يكسبون`
- Synthesis: Sealing does not end testimony; it reallocates the speaking role. Hands carry deeds, feet embody movement, and both become present witnesses. The lexical overlap between word and wound makes bodily history resemble a set of legible marks that cannot be overruled by the mouth.

### 13. P4 - Assigned Stations
- Semantic invariant: Judgment materializes as a fitted place, posture, company, and sensory state.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:55-58 installs the garden company in fruit, shade, couches, and peace, while 36:59 and 36:63-64 separate offenders into fire.
- Surprising reach: The two destinations are not abstract rewards but opposed spatial technologies: canopy and support versus cutting separation and heat-straightening.

#### Subchannel A. Sheltered repose and convivial provision
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Companions and spouses abide beneath a protective canopy, recline on fitted couches, eat fruit, exchange pleasant speech, and receive peace under sustaining care.
- Active motifs: companionship (quranic:root_000844:B001/m01); protection through company (quranic:root_000844:B002/m01); screened garden (quranic:root_000266:B003/m01); absorbing occupation (quranic:root_000801:B001/m01); delighted state (quranic:root_001174:B001/m01); pleasant fruit (quranic:root_001174:B002/m01); convivial speech (quranic:root_001174:B003/m01); marital pair (quranic:root_000652:B002/m01); shade (quranic:root_000966:B001/m01); overhead canopy (quranic:root_000966:B003/m01); protective shelter (quranic:root_000966:B004/m01); couch in an enclosure (quranic:root_000026:B004/m01); abiding in place (quranic:root_000026:B003/m01); supported reclining (quranic:root_001678:B004/m01); call that draws provision near (quranic:root_000478:B001/m01); safety (quranic:root_000737:B001/m01); peace opposed to war (quranic:root_000737:B004/m01); completing care (quranic:root_000532:B002/m01); compassionate mercy (quranic:root_000552:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:55 `أصحاب`, `الجنة`, `شغل`, `فاكهون`; 36:56 `أزواجهم`, `ظلال`, `الأرائك`, `متكئون`; 36:57 `فاكهة`, `يدعون`; 36:58 `سلام`, `قولا`, `رب`, `رحيم`
- Synthesis: The garden is assembled as a complete habitat: social company, paired household, canopy, couch, posture, food, call, speech, and care. Its invariant is supported ease, in which every spatial and social component preserves rather than exposes the occupant.

#### Subchannel B. Separation into punitive heat
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Offenders are cut out from the collective by their acquired offense and placed in fire whose heat seizes and reshapes.
- Active motifs: sorting from a mixed body (quranic:root_001461:B001/m01); separation as cutting (quranic:root_001461:B002/m01); severing operation (quranic:root_000239:B001/m01); acquired culpability (quranic:root_000239:B003/m01); crime and transgression (quranic:root_000239:B004/m01); encounter with fire (quranic:root_000880:B003/m01); heat used to straighten material (quranic:root_000880:B004/m01); covering and concealment (quranic:root_001307:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:59 `امتازوا`, `المجرمون`; 36:64 `اصلوها`, `تكفرون`
- Synthesis: Separation is both classificatory and cutting. What offenders have acquired identifies them, division removes them from the former body, and fire becomes the fitted medium of their new station, with heat carrying a severe material sense of straightening.

### 14. P4 - Covenant and Route
- Semantic invariant: A preserved allegiance keeps a traveler on an open, leveled route; rival allegiance tethers, bends, and displaces.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:60-62 moves from a prior covenant and worship to the straight path, hostile diversion, a great multitude, and failed reasoning.
- Surprising reach: Reason and worship both carry animal-management imagery: one can restrain by a guiding tether, while false service turns the road and traveler into subdued equipment.

#### Subchannel A. Preserved instruction on a leveled road
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A prior instruction is entrusted across generations, retained as covenant, enacted as service, and embodied in a traveled, upright road.
- Active motifs: maintained care over time (quranic:root_001055:B001/m01); entrusted injunction (quranic:root_001055:B002/m01); protected pact (quranic:root_001055:B003/m01); remembered prior encounter (quranic:root_001055:B004/m01); descendants from an origin (quranic:root_000156:B007/m01); obedient worship (quranic:root_000973:B003/m01); leveled traveled road (quranic:root_000973:B005/m01); straight path (quranic:root_000858:B001/m01); upright alignment (quranic:root_001273:B008/m01); reason that restrains ignorance (quranic:root_001036:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:60 `أعهد`, `بني`, `تعبدوا`; 36:61 `اعبدوني`, `صراط`, `مستقيم`; 36:62 `تعقلون`
- Synthesis: The covenant is a kept deposit rather than a momentary command. It travels through descendants, is enacted in service, and takes spatial form as a road made passable and straight; reason is the internal restraint that keeps the traveler from leaving it.

#### Subchannel B. Hostile tether and mass diversion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A remote rebel uses a strong crooked tether to pull a great ingrained mass from its route toward loss, disappearance, and false refuge.
- Active motifs: remoteness and severance (quranic:root_000796:B001/m01); long binding rope (quranic:root_000796:B002/m01); crooked resistance (quranic:root_000796:B003/m01); rebellious figure (quranic:root_000796:B004/m01); transgressive overstepping (quranic:root_000993:B001/m01); enemy relation (quranic:root_000993:B003/m01); side or bank that departs from the course (quranic:root_000993:B009/m01); deviation from a route (quranic:root_000913:B001/m01); disappearance into concealment (quranic:root_000913:B002/m01); loss of possession or location (quranic:root_000913:B003/m01); mountain-like multitude (quranic:root_000217:B002/m01); ingrained created nature (quranic:root_000217:B004/m01); coercion into an act (quranic:root_000217:B010/m01); numerical abundance (quranic:root_001286:B001/m01); animal tether (quranic:root_001036:B002/m01); fortified refuge (quranic:root_001036:B007/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:60 `الشيطان`, `عدو`, `مبين`; 36:62 `أضل`, `جبلا`, `كثيرا`, `تعقلون`
- Synthesis: Diversion has an opponent, instrument, mass, and destination. The enemy is lexically distant and crooked, the tether supplies control, the mountain-like multitude supplies resistant bulk, and misguidance shifts that mass from the road to a side-field where loss can masquerade as refuge.

### 15. P4 - Erasure of Navigational Agency
- Semantic invariant: Agency can be removed by deleting the sensor and track or by recasting the body so it cannot leave its assigned place.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:66 erases eyes and defeats racing on the path, while 36:67 transforms bodies in place and removes both advance and return.
- Surprising reach: Optical deletion, archival erasure, pathlessness, and bodily deformation are parallel ways of removing a recoverable route.

#### Subchannel A. Eye and track erased
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Visual light and the path's trace are wiped out, so competitive forward motion has neither sensor nor readable course.
- Active motifs: erased trace (quranic:root_000950:B001/m01); extinguished sight-light (quranic:root_000950:B002/m01); terrain without a path (quranic:root_000950:B004/m01); seeing eye (quranic:root_001069:B001/m01); racing advance (quranic:root_000671:B001/m01); failure to catch or reach (quranic:root_000671:B004/m01); straight path (quranic:root_000858:B001/m01); question of route and manner (quranic:root_000063:B005/m01); ocular sight (quranic:root_000121:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:66 `لطمسنا`, `أعينهم`, `فاستبقوا`, `الصراط`, `فأنى`, `يبصرون`
- Synthesis: The operation deletes both map and reader. The eye loses light, the ground loses its trace, and racing becomes futile because speed cannot compensate for a pathless field.

#### Subchannel B. Recast in place
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Bodily form, sensory quality, and vigor are altered at a fixed station until neither onward passage nor reversal remains possible.
- Active motifs: transformation into a worse form (quranic:root_001421:B001/m01); loss of taste or color (quranic:root_001421:B002/m01); bodily wasting (quranic:root_001421:B003/m01); fixed place or rank (quranic:root_001332:B002/m01); capacity for action (quranic:root_000956:B003/m01); passage and forward motion (quranic:root_001430:B001/m01); return to an earlier state (quranic:root_000544:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:67 `لمسخناهم`, `مكانتهم`, `استطاعوا`, `مضيا`, `يرجعون`
- Synthesis: Here the route remains conceptually available, but the traveler is altered. Recasting fixes the body to its station and removes the paired capacities that define navigation: proceeding and returning.

### 16. P5 - Message that Keeps Life Addressable
- Semantic invariant: The message gathers marks into intelligible remembrance and directs warning toward a recipient still capable of response.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:69 distinguishes recitation and reminder from poetry, and 36:70 aims warning at the living while fixing the word against denial.
- Surprising reach: The rejected category of poetry still contributes fine perception and marking, but those components are subordinated to recitation's more explicit assembly and disclosure.

#### Subchannel A. Marked remembrance rather than poetic fabrication
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Fine perception and visible marks are gathered into recitation, retained as remembrance, spoken, and made clear without becoming poetic craft.
- Active motifs: disclosed knowledge (quranic:root_001040:B001/m01); guiding mark (quranic:root_001040:B002/m01); fine awareness (quranic:root_000798:B005/m01); ritual or identifying sign (quranic:root_000798:B006/m01); composed poetry (quranic:root_000798:B007/m01); suitability for an operation (quranic:root_000138:B002/m01); retained memory (quranic:root_000516:B003/m01); spoken mention (quranic:root_000516:B004/m01); reminder restoring presence (quranic:root_000516:B009/m01); gathered articulation (quranic:root_001210:B001/m01); recitation (quranic:root_001210:B002/m01); clear appearance (quranic:root_000170:B004/m01); semantic disclosure (quranic:root_000170:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:69 `علمناه`, `الشعر`, `ينبغي`, `ذكر`, `قرآن`, `مبين`
- Synthesis: Poetry is excluded as the governing production mode, not because language lacks craft or perception. Fine awareness and signs remain, but recitation gathers them into a retained, speakable, and disclosing structure whose purpose is remembrance.

#### Subchannel B. Warning the living, fixing the verdict
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Warning seeks a living, benefit-capable recipient; refusal covers the truth until the uttered verdict becomes fixed and manifest.
- Active motifs: danger-awakening warning (quranic:root_001488:B001/m01); occurrence in time (quranic:root_001332:B001/m01); living being (quranic:root_000383:B003/m01); life as benefit and rescue (quranic:root_000383:B013/m01); truth matching reality (quranic:root_000347:B001/m01); binding obligation (quranic:root_000347:B002/m01); truth made manifest (quranic:root_000347:B005/m01); spoken utterance (quranic:root_001272:B001/m01); a thing's own signification (quranic:root_001272:B014/m01); defining limit (quranic:root_001272:B016/m01); truth covered (quranic:root_001307:B003/m01); benefit covered (quranic:root_001307:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:70 `لينذر`, `كان`, `حيا`, `يحق`, `القول`, `الكافرين`
- Synthesis: Life is defined functionally as remaining responsiveness and benefit. Warning addresses that openness; denial covers it, and the word then changes role from appeal to binding definition, fixing the denier's state in a verdict that matches what has become manifest.

### 17. P5 - Service and Ownership Reversal
- Semantic invariant: Real control is demonstrated by making a dependent system useful, whereas imagined control reverses when supposed protectors cannot serve and their clients become their mustered force.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:71-73 gives created, owned, tamed livestock and their uses, while 36:74-75 gives adopted gods that cannot help and a present army attached to them.
- Surprising reach: Gratitude is pictured as abundant milk after sparse feed, while false divine service exposes its worshipers as the only effective personnel in the arrangement.

#### Subchannel A. Tamed livestock as a service chain
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Created livestock passes under ownership, is made gently compliant and route-ready, and yields transport, food, drink, benefit, and visible increase.
- Active motifs: seeing and recognition (quranic:root_000531:B001/m01); created production (quranic:root_000434:B002/m01); manual workers (quranic:root_001046:B006/m01); owning hand (quranic:root_001693:B004/m01); livestock wealth (quranic:root_001525:B005/m01); property and control (quranic:root_001444:B002/m01); dominion (quranic:root_001444:B003/m01); gentle compliance (quranic:root_000519:B002/m01); taming after difficulty (quranic:root_000519:B003/m01); movement on a prepared course (quranic:root_000519:B004/m01); riding (quranic:root_000589:B001/m01); eating (quranic:root_000043:B001/m01); allotted food (quranic:root_000043:B003/m01); practical benefit (quranic:root_001536:B001/m01); drinking (quranic:root_000783:B001/m01); water share (quranic:root_000783:B002/m01); drinking place or vessel (quranic:root_000783:B004/m01); acknowledged favor (quranic:root_000810:B001/m01); visible yield from little input (quranic:root_000810:B002/m01); abundant milk (quranic:root_000810:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:71 `يروا`, `خلقنا`, `عملت`, `أيدينا`, `أنعاما`, `مالكون`; 36:72 `ذللناها`, `ركوبهم`, `يأكلون`; 36:73 `منافع`, `مشارب`, `يشكرون`
- Synthesis: The scene forms a complete husbandry economy: production, ownership, taming, route, rider, food, water-allocation, vessel, and benefit. Gratitude mirrors the animal that turns a small ration into visible body and abundant milk, closing the chain with responsive increase.

#### Subchannel B. Gods that cannot perform reciprocal service
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: People acquire other worshiped objects for rescue, but those objects supply neither aid, rain, nor gift; instead the worshipers form the only army present to the arrangement.
- Active motifs: acquired possession (quranic:root_000018:B010/m01); subordinate others (quranic:root_000502:B003/m01); worshiped object (quranic:root_000047:B001/m01); aid against an adversary (quranic:root_001510:B001/m01); rain-like rescue (quranic:root_001510:B004/m01); helping gift (quranic:root_001510:B005/m01); capacity for action (quranic:root_000956:B003/m01); mutually supporting army (quranic:root_000264:B001/m01); compelled presence (quranic:root_000333:B001/m01); mustered present group (quranic:root_000333:B007/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:74 `اتخذوا`, `من دون`, `آلهة`, `ينصرون`; 36:75 `يستطيعون`, `نصرهم`, `جند`, `محضرون`
- Synthesis: The ownership claim collapses under a service test. The gods cannot produce any mode of help, while the humans become the organized personnel attached to them. The relation reverses: those seeking servants are themselves the only mobilized servants.

### 18. P5 - Material Reconstitution
- Semantic invariant: Form can be inverted, generated from minimal fluid, repaired from fragments, or converted across apparently incompatible material states.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:68 reverses form through aging, 36:77 produces a disputant from a drop, 36:78-79 restores decayed bone, and 36:80 produces fire from green growth.
- Surprising reach: Decay vocabulary also supplies repair, and wet flourishing wood becomes both fuel and light-bearing blossom.

#### Subchannel A. Aging as inversion and relapse
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Extended life turns an organized form downward, relapses vigor into weakness, bows head and sight, and impairs both reason and gait.
- Active motifs: extended lifespan (quranic:root_001044:B001/m01); top-to-bottom inversion (quranic:root_001551:B001/m01); relapse after recovery (quranic:root_001551:B003/m01); diminished vigor (quranic:root_001551:B005/m01); bowed head and sight (quranic:root_001551:B006/m01); measured form (quranic:root_000434:B001/m01); balanced bodily shape (quranic:root_000434:B003/m01); ingrained temperament (quranic:root_000434:B004/m01); reason that restrains folly (quranic:root_001036:B001/m01); impaired legs (quranic:root_001036:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:68 `نعمره`, `ننكسه`, `الخلق`, `يعقلون`
- Synthesis: Aging is a systematic reversal, not a single loss. Duration turns upper to lower, recovered strength relapses, posture folds, gait degrades, and the reasoning faculty named as a restraint is itself placed under pressure.

#### Subchannel B. A drop becomes a manifest disputant
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A flowing reproductive drop is measured into visible human form, acquires perception, and emerges as an articulate adversarial side.
- Active motifs: manifest human being (quranic:root_000059:B001/m01); perception by sight or hearing (quranic:root_000059:B002/m01); human image in the eye (quranic:root_000059:B005/m01); generative water-drop (quranic:root_001518:B002/m01); flowing and dripping fluid (quranic:root_001518:B003/m01); created production (quranic:root_000434:B002/m01); completed bodily form (quranic:root_000434:B003/m01); adversarial disputant (quranic:root_000416:B001/m01); side or edge (quranic:root_000416:B002/m01); visible disclosure (quranic:root_000170:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:77 `ير`, `الإنسان`, `خلقناه`, `نطفة`, `خصيم`, `مبين`
- Synthesis: The transformation spans scale, medium, and social role. Fluid becomes bounded body, body becomes perceiving human, and the human takes a sharply defined adversarial side. The final disputant therefore manifests the very formative capacity he contests.

#### Subchannel C. Discarded bone repaired into life
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A speaker discards memory of his own formation and treats bone as worn fragments, but the same lexical field contains repair, re-creation, beginning, recurrence, and known return.
- Active motifs: spoken parable (quranic:root_001397:B003/m01); example as instructive sign (quranic:root_001397:B011/m01); loss of retained memory (quranic:root_001501:B001/m01); abandonment (quranic:root_001501:B002/m01); discarded remnant (quranic:root_001501:B003/m01); worn created form (quranic:root_000434:B009/m01); solid bone (quranic:root_001029:B005/m01); repair of what has decayed (quranic:root_000601:B001/m01); dust and fragments (quranic:root_000601:B003/m01); decayed remains (quranic:root_000601:B004/m01); living being (quranic:root_000383:B003/m01); re-creation (quranic:root_001502:B006/m01); first beginning (quranic:root_000067:B001/m01); return to outcome (quranic:root_000067:B002/m01); recurrence in time (quranic:root_001414:B002/m01); encompassing knowledge (quranic:root_001040:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:78 `ضرب`, `مثلا`, `نسي`, `خلقه`, `يحيي`, `العظام`, `رميم`; 36:79 `يحييها`, `أنشأها`, `أول`, `مرة`, `خلق`, `عليم`
- Synthesis: The objection depends on classifying remains as discarded and irrecoverable. Yet the decay root also names repair of a wall or ruined affair; beginning and recurrence supply the temporal operation, and knowledge preserves the form that fragments no longer visibly display.

#### Subchannel D. Green wood converted to flame
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Moist green growth and load-bearing wood are transformed into fuel, hearth, and active flame.
- Active motifs: change into a new state (quranic:root_000248:B002/m01); tree with a trunk (quranic:root_000777:B001/m01); load-bearing interlocked wood (quranic:root_000777:B004/m01); fresh green plant (quranic:root_000418:B002/m01); moist flourishing life (quranic:root_000418:B003/m01); burning fire (quranic:root_001564:B002/m01); tree blossom as light (quranic:root_001564:B004/m01); ignition (quranic:root_001672:B001/m01); fuel material (quranic:root_001672:B002/m01); hearth (quranic:root_001672:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:80 `جعل`, `الشجر`, `الأخضر`, `نارا`, `توقدون`
- Synthesis: The materials appear incompatible because green growth carries moisture and flourishing life. The scene nevertheless supplies a conversion chain from tree structure to fuel, place of burning, and flame; even the tree's blossom contributes a latent light-bearing stage before combustion.

### 19. P5 - Sovereign Actualization and Return
- Semantic invariant: The capacity that designs and commands forms also holds their total field together and determines their return.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:81 argues from cosmic creation and capacity, 36:82 makes will and command produce being, and 36:83 locates all dominion in a powerful hand and directs all things back.
- Surprising reach: The command is simultaneously affair, imperative, authority, and road-marker; the hand is simultaneously power, ownership, and executable rule.

#### Subchannel A. Cosmic capacity by measured analogy
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The maker who measured and produced sky and earth is tested by analogy for capacity to make their counterpart.
- Active motifs: overhead sky (quranic:root_000745:B004/m01); earth below the sky (quranic:root_000025:B001/m01); measured design before action (quranic:root_000434:B001/m01); origination (quranic:root_000434:B002/m01); effective capacity and possession (quranic:root_001205:B003/m01); measured planning (quranic:root_001205:B005/m01); likeness (quranic:root_001397:B001/m01); negation set aside (quranic:root_001390:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:81 `أوليس`, `خلق`, `السماوات`, `الأرض`, `بقادر`, `يخلق`, `مثلهم`
- Synthesis: The analogy preserves operation rather than scale: measured design and origination have already produced the larger field, so capacity for a corresponding form follows from the demonstrated making relation.

#### Subchannel B. Performative command
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Sovereign will selects an object, articulates an imperative, and the object occurs in time as the command's realized state.
- Active motifs: affair or state (quranic:root_000051:B001/m01); binding imperative (quranic:root_000051:B002/m01); governing authority (quranic:root_000051:B003/m01); directed will (quranic:root_000610:B001/m01); knowable object (quranic:root_000831:B001/m01); will attached to an object (quranic:root_000831:B002/m01); articulated utterance (quranic:root_001272:B001/m01); a thing's own signification (quranic:root_001272:B014/m01); occurrence in time (quranic:root_001332:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:82 `أمره`, `أراد`, `شيئا`, `يقول`, `كن`, `فيكون`
- Synthesis: The scene is a complete performative sequence: authority possesses an affair, will selects its object, utterance gives the object a directive sign, and being is the temporal occurrence of that directive rather than a separate fabrication stage.

#### Subchannel C. Total custody and return
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Power, ownership, and authority cohere in one hand that sustains the total domain and receives every thing back at its destination.
- Active motifs: transcendence from defect (quranic:root_000666:B002/m01); hand as power (quranic:root_001693:B002/m01); hand as ownership (quranic:root_001693:B004/m01); hand as executable authority (quranic:root_001693:B005/m01); cohesion that keeps a structure standing (quranic:root_001444:B001/m01); possession and disposal (quranic:root_001444:B002/m01); dominion and rule (quranic:root_001444:B003/m01); governing support of an affair (quranic:root_001444:B005/m01); encompassing totality (quranic:root_001315:B003/m01); return to origin (quranic:root_000544:B001/m01); destination and final outcome (quranic:root_000067:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:79 `أول`; 36:83 `فسبحان`, `بيده`, `ملكوت`, `كل`, `شيء`, `إليه`, `ترجعون`
- Synthesis: The hand is not a single metaphorical feature but a convergence of force, possession, and enforceable rule. Dominion supplies the cohesion by which the whole remains a whole; return then closes custody by bringing each object to the outcome already contained within that rule.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. P1 - Mercy as a protective interior and repaired surface
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Awe before an unseen source is received into womb-like mercy, covered from damaging consequence, repaired, and made visibly glad and honored.
- Active motifs: awe-filled fear (quranic:root_000413:B001/m01); hiddenness from sight (quranic:root_001117:B001/m01); preserving cover (quranic:root_001096:B001/m01); forgiveness shielding from consequence (quranic:root_001096:B002/m01); compassionate mercy (quranic:root_000552:B001/m01); womb as protective interior (quranic:root_000552:B003/m01); news opening the skin with joy (quranic:root_000120:B005/m01); bestowed honor (quranic:root_001294:B001/m01); crooked break repaired (quranic:root_000015:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:5 `الرحيم`; 36:11 `خشي`, `الرحمن`, `بالغيب`, `فبشره`, `بمغفرة`, `أجر`, `كريم`
- Synthesis: Mercy and forgiveness form a layered shelter: a womb-like interior receives the one who responds without sight, and a preserving cover blocks the aftereffects of fault. Reward contributes the concrete image of a badly set break repaired, while glad news and honor move the result outward onto the recipient's visible surface.

### S2. P2 - Courier from the periphery
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A knowledgeable local crosses the distance from a city's far edge on foot, expends purposeful effort, and leads others toward an unpaid guide.
- Active motifs: farthest edge (quranic:root_001235:B001/m01); walled collective city (quranic:root_001408:B001/m01); local who knows the city's affairs (quranic:root_001408:B004/m01); leg as locomotor member (quranic:root_000546:B001/m01); walking without a mount (quranic:root_000546:B003/m01); directed quick movement (quranic:root_000709:B001/m01); effort and work (quranic:root_000709:B002/m01); following in another's wake (quranic:root_000175:B001/m01); wage returned for work (quranic:root_000015:B001/m01); gentle route-guidance (quranic:root_001583:B001/m01); leading element at the front (quranic:root_001583:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:20 `أقصا`, `المدينة`, `رجل`, `يسعى`, `اتبعوا`; 36:21 `اتبعوا`, `يسألكم`, `أجرا`, `مهتدون`
- Synthesis: The scene has a mapped origin, a pedestrian body, exertion, and a social destination. The courier's local knowledge and movement from the margin toward the center authorize his guidance, while refusal of wages removes a competing transactional motive from the route he recommends.

### S3. P2 - One cry extinguishes a deployment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A potential heavenly military descent is withheld; one alarming signal replaces army and duel, quenching both flame and collective life.
- Active motifs: mutually supporting army (quranic:root_000264:B001/m01); stony military ground (quranic:root_000264:B002/m01); overhead sky (quranic:root_000745:B004/m01); delivered descent (quranic:root_001492:B002/m01); descent into combat (quranic:root_001492:B007/m01); alarming cry (quranic:root_000895:B002/m01); numerical one (quranic:root_001631:B002/m01); flame stilled over embers (quranic:root_000437:B001/m01); life or awareness stilled after motion (quranic:root_000437:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:28 `أنزلنا`, `جند`, `السماء`, `منزلين`; 36:29 `صيحة`, `واحدة`, `خامدون`
- Synthesis: The expected force package would descend from above, occupy hard ground, and enter combat. Instead a single acoustic event performs the entire operation without deployment: its result is described through fire whose flame has gone still and, by extension, a population whose animation has ceased.

### S4. P3 - A cry seizes an unfinished social transfer
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: People wait on a promised event while arguing; one cry captures them before testament, household transfer, or return can be completed.
- Active motifs: promise opening expectation (quranic:root_001662:B001/m01); threat (quranic:root_001662:B002/m01); appointed time (quranic:root_001662:B003/m01); truthful statement (quranic:root_000852:B001/m01); fulfillment in action (quranic:root_000852:B004/m01); waiting for time (quranic:root_001520:B002/m01); alarming cry (quranic:root_000895:B002/m01); numerical one (quranic:root_001631:B002/m01); taking into possession (quranic:root_000018:B001/m01); capture (quranic:root_000018:B003/m01); adversarial dispute (quranic:root_000416:B001/m01); capacity for action (quranic:root_000956:B003/m01); connection of one thing to another (quranic:root_001656:B001/m01); testament transmitted onward (quranic:root_001656:B002/m01); household and kin (quranic:root_000064:B001/m01); return to an originating place (quranic:root_000544:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:48 `يقولون`, `الوعد`, `صادقين`; 36:49 `ينظرون`, `صيحة`, `واحدة`, `تأخذهم`, `يخصمون`; 36:50 `يستطيعون`, `توصية`, `أهلهم`, `يرجعون`
- Synthesis: Expectation is not resolved through deliberation. The event arrives as a seizure while social speech is still adversarial, cutting the connective operations by which property, instruction, and presence would pass to a household. The interruption is therefore temporal, bodily, and intergenerational at once.

### S5. P5 - Hidden and public speech under knowledge
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Spoken claims produce grief, but unspoken inner content and public declaration occupy one field of complete knowledge.
- Active motifs: roughened inner state of grief (quranic:root_000317:B001/m01); spoken utterance (quranic:root_001272:B001/m01); unspoken saying in the self (quranic:root_001272:B012/m01); disclosed knowledge (quranic:root_001040:B001/m01); concealed inward matter (quranic:root_000697:B001/m01); pure inner core (quranic:root_000697:B005/m01); access to hidden affairs (quranic:root_000697:B014/m01); public manifestation (quranic:root_001041:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:76 `يحزنك`, `قولهم`, `نعلم`, `يسرون`, `يعلنون`
- Synthesis: Speech is divided by visibility, not by knowability. Public utterance can roughen the hearer's interior, while unsaid content remains an inward saying with its own core; knowledge crosses both surfaces and removes the informational advantage of concealment.

### S6. Skinning and tanning a measured hide
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A hide is peeled from the body, kept hair-on when desired, and treated with a small measured charge of tanning bark or leaves.
- Active motifs: hide stripped from a body (quranic:root_000729:B001/m01); hair retained on hide through skinning or tanning (quranic:root_000844:B006/m01); small measured dose of tannin (quranic:root_001533:B007/m01); acacia bark or leaves used for tanning (quranic:root_000737:B008/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:13 `أصحاب`; 36:36 `أنفسهم`; 36:37 `نسلخ`; 36:58 `سلام`
- Synthesis: This craft sequence reframes the stripping of day in 36:37 as the opening operation of a controlled material transformation. A removed covering need not be mere waste: measured treatment preserves it as usable hide, supporting the Surah's wider movement from exhausted or concealed matter into a new functional state.

### S7. Stitching and seasoning a waterskin
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Leather pieces are stitched into a vessel with side panels, weak apertures are found, clay and water are worked through the skin to seal its seams, and its mouth is tied shut.
- Active motifs: stitching leather into a waterskin (quranic:root_001283:B001/m02); side panels of a leather water bag (quranic:root_001536:B002/m01); thin spot or leaking eye in a waterskin (quranic:root_001069:B007/m01); seasoning with clay and water to seal stitches (quranic:root_000783:B013/m01); tie closing the vessel's mouth (quranic:root_001678:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:12 `نكتب`; 36:34 `عيون`; 36:56 `متكئون`; 36:73 `منافع`, `مشارب`
- Synthesis: The scene materializes the Surah's springs, benefits, and drinking places as provision that can be carried. Water becomes reliably available only after separate pieces are joined, leaks are closed, taste is conditioned, and the opening is controlled.

### S8. Drawing water through a timbered wellhead
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A timber-and-stone wellhead carries a rotating axle; a spliced, long-twisted rope reaches the water, bears a one-handled bucket by its lug, and passes between drawers taking turns.
- Active motifs: stone and paired timbers at a wellhead (quranic:root_000547:B006/m01); rotating iron axle of a pulley (quranic:root_000610:B006/m01); rope splice extending a line to water (quranic:root_000471:B003/m01); long twisted well-rope (quranic:root_000796:B002/m02); one-handled drawing bucket (quranic:root_000737:B011/m01); bucket lug or load-balancing side piece (quranic:root_000741:B008/m01); water drawers alternating turns (quranic:root_000433:B011/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:9 `خلف`; 36:18 `نرجم`; 36:23 `يرد`; 36:25 `اسمعون`; 36:40 `تدرك`; 36:58 `سلام`; 36:60 `الشيطان`
- Synthesis: This apparatus supports the dead-earth and spring reading by making provision a vertical transfer rather than a bare appearance of water. The source may be given, but usable access still requires joined lengths, balanced load, rotation, and coordinated turn-taking.

### S9. Gestation, birth, and afterbirth
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A womb encloses an inward pregnancy and hidden fetus; birth releases the child with blood and afterbirth, while the womb remains subject to postpartum pain.
- Active motifs: womb as the enclosed growth chamber (quranic:root_000552:B003/m01); fetus hidden in the mother's abdomen (quranic:root_000266:B007/m01); inward pregnancy carried in the body (quranic:root_000357:B002/m01); child emergence with postpartum blood (quranic:root_001533:B005/m01); matter expelled with the child at birth (quranic:root_000822:B006/m01); postpartum pain or swelling of the womb (quranic:root_000552:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:5 `الرحيم`; 36:26 `الجنة`; 36:36 `أنفسهم`; 36:41 `حملنا`; 36:65 `تشهد`
- Synthesis: The sequence supports the Surah's creation-and-return argument by making emergence from concealment a full bodily process: enclosure, formation, exit, residue, and continuing consequence. The disputant made from a drop is thus preceded by an unseen material history rather than appearing as an unmediated adult body.

### S10. Timed milking and churned yield
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Milking is scheduled, approached from a designated side, and performed with the fingertips; retained milk draws the next flow, a full udder yields repeatedly, and churning gathers butter before the liquid thickens.
- Active motifs: appointed time for milking (quranic:root_000382:B003/m01); milker positioned at a particular side (quranic:root_000170:B009/m01); fingertip milking (quranic:root_001165:B005/m01); milk left in the udder to draw the next flow (quranic:root_000478:B003/m01); full udder and abundant milk (quranic:root_000810:B003/m01); repeated flow of milk (quranic:root_000563:B006/m01); butter gathering in the skin or churn (quranic:root_000205:B004/m01); milk thickening at the end of processing (quranic:root_000067:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:3 `المرسلين`; 36:9 `بين`; 36:22 `فطرني`; 36:35 `ثمره`, `يشكرون`; 36:44 `حين`; 36:57 `يدعون`; 36:79 `أول`
- Synthesis: The scene supports 36:71-73 by expanding livestock drink into a timed husbandry and processing chain. Possession alone does not produce nourishment: position, touch, retained yield, repeated flow, and churning cooperate before milk becomes a stable food, giving gratitude a concrete economy of care and work.

### S11. Letting the young animal nurse or blocking its tongue
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A suckling young animal may be released with its mother to nurse freely, or fitted with a small rod or spindle-like piece on its tongue until nursing stops.
- Active motifs: young animal sent with its mother to nurse at will (quranic:root_000546:B014/m01); tongue device preventing a young animal from suckling (quranic:root_001177:B006/m01); withholding and weaning from a desired thing (quranic:root_000994:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:18 `عذاب`; 36:20 `رجل`; 36:40 `فلك`
- Synthesis: This husbandry choice usefully pressures the statement that humans drink from livestock in 36:73. The human benefit is produced by deciding whether maternal milk reaches the offspring or is redirected, so domestication appears as an intervention into feeding rather than neutral availability.

### S12. Lowering the cooking pot and feeding a guest
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Food is cooked in a pot, the hot vessel is lowered from the fire with a protective cloth, its contents pass to an eating bowl, and the prepared meal is offered to a guest.
- Active motifs: cooking pot and the cook who tends it (quranic:root_001205:B007/m01); cloth used to lower a hot pot (quranic:root_000248:B007/m01); bowl or vessel from which food is eaten (quranic:root_000043:B013/m01); gathered food offered in hospitality (quranic:root_001222:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:8 `جعلنا`; 36:13 `القرية`; 36:35 `يأكلون`; 36:38 `تقدير`
- Synthesis: The scene materializes the Surah's grain, fruit, eating, and hospitality as one social conversion. Growth becomes nourishment through heat and careful handling, then becomes welcome only when the finished food crosses from cooking vessel to guest.

### S13. Facing, standing, and completing prayer
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A worshipper deliberately turns toward a prayer direction, stands, completes the sequence of standing, bowing, and prostration, and joins bodily obedience to praise.
- Active motifs: deliberate aiming and orientation (quranic:root_000053:B012/m01); direction faced in prayer (quranic:root_001198:B005/m01); bodily standing (quranic:root_001273:B002/m01); formal prayer with standing, bowing, and prostration (quranic:root_000880:B001/m01); praise enacted as prayer (quranic:root_000666:B001/m01); obedient worship (quranic:root_000973:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:4 `مستقيم`; 36:12 `إمام`; 36:22 `أعبد`; 36:31 `قبل`; 36:36 `سبحان`; 36:61 `اعبدوني`, `مستقيم`; 36:64 `اصلوا`
- Synthesis: This rite supports 36:61 by turning worship on a straight path into calibrated bodily orientation. Intention selects a direction, standing establishes the body's axis, and the completed movements make obedience and praise spatially enacted rather than only inward assent.

### S14. A staked race with a ranked finish
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Competitors race for a named stake; one advances first, a second horse follows close behind, and a third completes the ranked order.
- Active motifs: racing advance (quranic:root_000671:B001/m01); prize or stake set for a race (quranic:root_000671:B002/m01); second horse following the winner (quranic:root_000880:B007/m01); horse arriving third in the race (quranic:root_000203:B003/m02)
- Ayah anchors: 36:14 `ثالث`; 36:40 `سابق`; 36:64 `اصلوا`
- Synthesis: The race script usefully pressures 36:40: ordinary racing expects a stake, overtaking, and ranks produced by displacement. The verse denies that competitive outcome between sun and moon, converting shared motion from a contest for precedence into coordinated traffic.

### S15. Earning release through a written manumission
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person held as transferable property enters a written emancipation contract and works through its assessed payments until ownership is dissolved and the neck is freed.
- Active motifs: property held under an owner's control (quranic:root_001444:B002/m01); person owned as a slave (quranic:root_000973:B001/m01); action of reducing a person to servitude (quranic:root_000973:B004/m01); written manumission contract with staged payments (quranic:root_001283:B005/m01); slave's earnings applied to the price of release (quranic:root_000709:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:12 `نكتب`; 36:20 `يسعى`; 36:22 `أعبد`; 36:60 `تعبدوا`; 36:71 `مالكون`; 36:83 `ملكوت`
- Synthesis: This social transaction usefully pressures a flat equation of worship with chattel slavery. Worldly bondage has an owner, a price, a written schedule, and a negotiated exit; 36:60-61 instead frames worship as covenantal allegiance whose direction is the straight path.

### S16. Struck lute and modulated song
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A wooden lute or similar instrument is struck with its implement while a singer shapes a pleasurable vocal line through repeated, modulated phrases received in listening.
- Active motifs: wooden lute or struck musical instrument (quranic:root_001058:B010/m01); implement used to strike a lute (quranic:root_000906:B014/m01); singing voice and song (quranic:root_001110:B003/m01); vocal modulation in reading, song, or the call to prayer (quranic:root_000544:B007/m01); pleasurable sung sound and its performer (quranic:root_000741:B007/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:13 `اضرب`; 36:23 `تغن`; 36:25 `اسمعون`; 36:39 `عاد`; 36:83 `ترجعون`
- Synthesis: The performance scene usefully pressures a purely acoustic reading of the denial of poetry in 36:69. Returned vocal phrasing can belong to recitation, the call to prayer, or song, while instrument, striking tool, singer, and pleasurable listening complete a distinct entertainment frame; the reminder is therefore distinguished by communicative function, not by an absence of patterned sound.

### S17. Kindling, feeding, and quenching a fire
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A rod is worked inside a hollow fire-stick until it catches; the resulting sparks are tested for usefulness, fuel is fed to the flame, and embers are finally buried to make the fire die down.
- Active motifs: hollow fire-stick receiving a kindling rod (quranic:root_000697:B008/m01); fire-stick catching rapidly (quranic:root_001672:B005/m01); weak unusable sparks (quranic:root_000286:B011/m01); fire consuming fed wood (quranic:root_000043:B005/m01); ember-burying place for quenching fire (quranic:root_000437:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:29 `خامدون`; 36:33 `حبا`, `يأكل`; 36:35,72 `يأكل`; 36:76 `يسر`; 36:80 `توقدون`
- Synthesis: The scene directly materializes 36:80 beyond the first appearance of flame from green wood. Latent heat becomes usable fire through fitted tools, successful ignition, spark discrimination, and added fuel; burial of the embers then shows the same force brought back under control.

### S18. Grave concealment and the tethered burial camel
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The dead person is shrouded and hidden in a grave marked by gathered stones, while a camel is tethered beside its owner and denied food and water until it too dies.
- Active motifs: shrouding and burying the dead (quranic:root_000266:B009/m01); concealing a body in a grave (quranic:root_001117:B009/m01); piled grave stones (quranic:root_000547:B005/m01); grave-camel tethered without food or water (quranic:root_000154:B006/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:11 `بالغيب`; 36:18 `لنرجمنكم`; 36:26,34,55 `الجنة`, `جنات`; surface anchor unavailable for root `ب ل ي`
- Synthesis: The rite usefully pressures the grave-exit of 36:51. Human burial conceals the body, fixes its place with stone, and extends death to a living possession beside it; the horn reverses that whole arrangement by making grave-bound bodies mobile at once.

### S19. Spinning, weaving, and seaming a covering
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A spindle whorl turns and twists yarn; opposed strands are joined according to a weaving model, woven firmly, and sewn edge to edge into a strong finished cloth.
- Active motifs: spindle whorl (quranic:root_001177:B005/m01); joined opposing yarn elements (quranic:root_001198:B010/m01); model on which cloth is woven (quranic:root_000906:B007/m01); well-spun and tightly woven fabric (quranic:root_000217:B007/m01); seam joining two cloth edges (quranic:root_000121:B006/m01); strong cloth made from abundant yarn (quranic:root_000043:B012/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:9,66 `يبصرون`; 36:13,78 `اضرب`, `ضرب`; 36:31 `قبلهم`; 36:33,35,72 `يأكلون`; 36:40-41 `فلك`; 36:62 `جبلا`
- Synthesis: This fabrication sequence reframes 36:37 by supplying the reverse operation to stripping away a covering. Aligned strands, a governing pattern, tight interlacing, and closed seams produce the surface that stripping would remove, making the day-night reversal legible as an undoing of organized material.

### S20. Estimating and raising a timbered frame
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A builder estimates the needed masonry, shapes wood with an adze, joins the structure, raises its supports, seats the pillar head under cross-timbers, lays transverse beams, and closes remaining gaps.
- Active motifs: estimating the number of bricks needed (quranic:root_000950:B007/m01); adze used to carve wood (quranic:root_001207:B008/m01); building by joining parts (quranic:root_000156:B001/m01); house supports and pillars (quranic:root_000156:B009/m01); timbers carrying a pillar head (quranic:root_001222:B008/m01); transverse roof or door beam (quranic:root_001001:B013/m01); material used to close an opening (quranic:root_000687:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:9 `سدا`; 36:12,39 `قدموا`, `القديم`; 36:13 `القرية`; 36:46 `معرضين`; 36:60 `بني`; 36:66 `لطمسنا`
- Synthesis: The scene materializes the barrier of 36:9 as an engineered closure. Estimation, cutting, joining, bracing, spanning, and plugging turn obstruction from a bare object into the outcome of a measured construction sequence.

### S21. Fitting, drawing, and delivering an arrow
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A smooth shaft receives its point, waits in a quiver joined to the bow, is taken by hand, set against the bow limb, drawn to the limit, and sent to its intended endpoint.
- Active motifs: smoothed arrow shaft (quranic:root_000434:B008/m01); arrowhead fitted into its shaft (quranic:root_000589:B004/m01); quiver joined to a bow (quranic:root_001221:B007/m01); hand reaching into the quiver for an arrow (quranic:root_000544:B010/m01); bow limb (quranic:root_000546:B009/m01); bow drawn to its fullest extent (quranic:root_001080:B004/m01); arrow or message reaching its target or addressee (quranic:root_001560:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:18 `تنتهوا`; 36:20,65 `رجل`, `أرجلهم`; 36:22,31,50,67,83 forms of `رجع`; 36:31 `القرون`; 36:36,42,68,71,77-79,81 forms of `خلق`; 36:42,72 `يركبون`, `ركوبهم`; 36:43 `نغرقهم`
- Synthesis: The final motif makes directed delivery the invariant shared by projectile and report. The assembled bow scene therefore usefully pressures the Surah's sent-message reading: a warning reaches an addressee by intelligible speech, while the surrounding threats imagine directed force reaching a body.

### S22. Moonlit hunting and verifying the kill
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Hunters go out under moonlight when prey vision is compromised, set snares or use a feeding bow or trained raptor, strike successive animals, and inspect the fallen quarry to determine whether it has died.
- Active motifs: hunters setting out in pursuit (quranic:root_000745:B006/m01); moonlit hunting against visually impaired prey (quranic:root_001255:B003/m01); snares and traps set for game (quranic:root_000880:B005/m01); hunting tool or raptor that feeds its owner (quranic:root_000934:B006/m01); successive prey taken in one discharge (quranic:root_000993:B008/m01); inspection to establish whether prey is dead (quranic:root_001454:B014/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:12,33 `الموتى`, `ميتة`; 36:28,81 `السماء`, `السماوات`; 36:39-40 `القمر`; 36:47 `نطعم`, `أطعم`; 36:60 `عدو`; 36:64 `اصلوها`
- Synthesis: The hunt ends not with self-evident death but with a human examination of an uncertain body. That uncertainty usefully pressures 36:79: the hunter must infer whether life has ceased, whereas the one who first created the body knows its state and can restore it.

### S23. A wound that suppurates, heals, and relapses
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A boil or ulcer emerges, fills with pus, swells and spreads toward corruption, then settles as healthy flesh reappears, yet remains capable of returning to a worse state.
- Active motifs: boil or ulcer emerging from the body (quranic:root_000400:B004/m01); pus accumulated in a wound (quranic:root_000282:B003/m01); wound swelling and spreading into corruption (quranic:root_000138:B004/m01); swelling subsiding as healthy flesh appears (quranic:root_000026:B005/m01); wound relapsing after improvement (quranic:root_001096:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:11,27 `مغفرة`, `غفر`; 36:13,20 `جاء`; 36:33 `أخرجنا`; 36:40,69 `ينبغي`; 36:56 `الأرائك`
- Synthesis: The bodily sequence supports the Surah's repair imagery while marking the limits of ordinary repair. Human tissue can reorganize and look sound, but the improvement remains reversible; renewed creation in 36:78-81 is presented as decisive rather than a precarious remission.

### S24. Protecting, ripening, and gathering dates
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Palms stand in planted rows; the blossom and young fruit remain sheathed, the first dates ripen and acquire flavor, the crop becomes accessible to picking, and fresh fruit is gathered into a woven palm-leaf basket before gleanings remain after harvest.
- Active motifs: palms planted in rows (quranic:root_000589:B009/m01); sheath enclosing palm blossom or fruit (quranic:root_001307:B010/m01); first ripe dates appearing (quranic:root_000120:B007/m01); fruit reaching maturity and flavor (quranic:root_000934:B005/m01); ripe palm fruit ready to gather (quranic:root_000956:B007/m01); woven palm-leaf basket for fresh dates (quranic:root_000464:B010/m01); fallen gleanings and dry dates after harvest (quranic:root_000239:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:11,15 `فبشره`, `بشر`; 36:26 `ادخل`; 36:42,72 `يركبون`, `ركوبهم`; 36:47 `نطعم`, `أطعم`, `كفروا`; 36:50,67,75 forms of `استطاع`; 36:59 `المجرمون`; 36:64,70 forms of `كفر`
- Synthesis: The scene directly supports the palms and fruit of 36:34-35 by restoring their temporal and material chain. Sheathing and ripening belong to growth, while picking, basketry, and gleaning belong to human handling; the distinction sharpens the Surah's refusal to treat produced life as simply the work of human hands.

### S25. Opening, administering, and discharging an oath
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A speaker invokes the divine name, opens an oath with its confirming particle, swears, may administer the oath to reassure or test another person, narrows its scope by exception, and answers breach with expiation.
- Active motifs: divine name used in an oath formula (quranic:root_000047:B002/m01); confirming particle that opens an oath (quranic:root_000074:B010/m01); oath and swearing (quranic:root_000076:B007/m01); oath administered for reassurance or testing (quranic:root_000154:B005/m01); exception removing one item from an oath's scope (quranic:root_000208:B008/m01); expiation covering a broken oath (quranic:root_001307:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:14 `اثنين`; 36:23,47,74 forms of `الله`, `آلهة`; 36:33,37,41,46 `آية`, `آيات`; 36:47,64,70 forms of `كفر`; surface anchors unavailable for roots `ء ل ي` and `ب ل ي`
- Synthesis: The social frame supports the oath that opens the Surah by treating sworn speech as an accountable act rather than ornament. Invocation, confirmation, administration, exception, and expiation trace how an utterance creates an obligation and how its failure must be answered.

### S26. Appraising and closing a cash sale
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Merchandise is exposed for inspection, assigned a price, paid for in present full-weight coin handed directly between parties, and carried through as a completed sale.
- Active motifs: merchandise displayed for sale (quranic:root_001001:B002/m01); appraisal and pricing of goods (quranic:root_001273:B010/m01); coin at exact standard weight (quranic:root_001273:B015/m01); ready cash rather than absent debt (quranic:root_001069:B011/m01); hand-to-hand cash payment (quranic:root_001693:B007/m01); sale ratified and allowed to proceed (quranic:root_001430:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:4,6,19-20,26,28,61 forms of `قوم`, `مستقيم`; 36:9,35,45,65,71,83 forms of `يد`; 36:34,66 `عيون`, `أعين`; 36:46 `معرضين`; 36:67 `مضيا`
- Synthesis: The transaction reframes the Surah's repeated hands through an ordinary mechanism of acquisition. A hand can close an exchange and establish possession, but 36:35 denies that such hands made the fruit, and 36:65 reverses their commercial agency by making them testify about their owner.

### S27. Contracting a marriage and establishing a household
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A marriage is contracted, the bride-price is assigned, the bride is conveyed to her husband, a wedding shelter is raised for entry into married life, and a new household relation is established.
- Active motifs: witnessed marriage contract (quranic:root_001444:B004/m01); bride-price (quranic:root_000852:B007/m01); bride conveyed to her husband (quranic:root_001583:B006/m01); wedding shelter and entry into marriage (quranic:root_000156:B006/m01); taking a spouse and forming a household (quranic:root_000064:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:21 `مهتدون`; 36:48,52 `صادقين`, `صدق`; 36:50 `أهلهم`; 36:60 `بني`; 36:71,83 `مالكون`, `ملكوت`
- Synthesis: The sequence materializes the pairing of 36:36 and the spouses of 36:56 as a social passage rather than abstract symmetry. Contract, gift, transfer, shelter, and household convert two persons into a publicly situated pair.

### S28. Warranty, surety, and recourse
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A written purchase warranty preserves recourse for defects; liability follows the transaction, a guarantor or surety assumes the right or fine, and a claimant invokes the obligation against the responsible party.
- Active motifs: written purchase warranty and defect condition (quranic:root_001055:B006/m01); liability following a sale (quranic:root_000471:B004/m01); guarantor's undertaking (quranic:root_001198:B008/m01); surety carrying a right or fine (quranic:root_000357:B004/m01); claimant pursuing money or another right (quranic:root_000175:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:11,20-21 forms of `تبع`; 36:31 `قبلهم`; 36:40 `تدرك`; 36:41 `حملنا`; 36:60 `أعهد`
- Synthesis: This legal sequence materializes the Surah's record and settlement language in 36:12 and 36:54. Human recourse needs a deed, allocated liability, a substitute bearer, and an active claimant; eschatological settlement retains exact obligation while removing dependence on those fallible intermediaries.

### S29. Divorce, family return, and renewed courtship
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Divorce may sever the right of marital return or permit reconciliation; the woman may instead return to her family, after which suitors communicate with her as a widow or divorcee.
- Active motifs: irrevocable divorce cutting off marital return (quranic:root_000170:B012/m01); husband taking his wife back after divorce (quranic:root_000544:B004/m01); woman returning to her family after divorce or widowhood (quranic:root_000544:B004/m02); suitors corresponding with a widowed or divorced woman (quranic:root_000563:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 36:3,13-14,16,20,30,52 forms of `رسل`; 36:9,12,17,24,45,47,60,69,77 forms of `بين`; 36:22,31,50,67,83 forms of `رجع`
- Synthesis: The scene sharpens the interruption in 36:50. Return to one's family is normally a meaningful social transition after a household bond ends, and renewed correspondence can open another future; the seizing cry cancels even that available route before it can begin.

## Cross-Pericope Parent Channels

### 20. Signal efficacy across state change
- Semantic invariant: A directed signal joins source, transmission, recipient, and outcome, while its efficacy is measured by the state change produced in the addressed field.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:2-11 and 36:13-29 establish transmitted warning, 36:48-53 moves through seizing cry and gathering blast, 36:69-70 restores the warning function, and 36:82 makes uttered command coincide with realized being.
- Pericopes and bridge: P1, P2, P3, P4, and P5; causal role progression with reversal. Warning first seeks voluntary hearing, the single cry then terminates motion and unfinished transfer, the gathering signal reverses stillness into presence, and sovereign address finally produces its object.
- Canonical scene placements: P1 1A, "Dispatch onto a straight route", and 1B, "Tracking a reminder beyond sight"; P2 4A, "A relay completed by reinforcement", and S3, "One cry extinguishes a deployment"; P3 S4, "A cry seizes an unfinished social transfer"; P4 11B, "One signal gathers all"; P5 16A, "Marked remembrance rather than poetic fabrication", 16B, "Warning the living, fixing the verdict", and 19B, "Performative command".
- Surprising reach: The recipient progresses from responsible hearer to seized population, reassembled collective, and commanded object; acoustic or verbal form stays recognizable while causal force changes scale.
- Synthesis: The same source-signal-recipient-outcome architecture governs all five pericopes. Human response matters at the warning stage, but later signals progressively remove delay and negotiation: one arrests, one gathers, and one actualizes.

### 21. Route agency and compelled return
- Semantic invariant: A route scene couples orientation, a readable course, capacity for motion, impediment, and destination; its decisive variable is who controls passage.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:4-9 sets straightness against bodily and visual obstruction, 36:20-21 gives purposeful pedestrian traversal, 36:38-40 assigns measured celestial courses, 36:60-67 contrasts covenantal direction with diversion and erased passage, and 36:83 fixes final return.
- Pericopes and bridge: P1, P2, P3, P4, and P5; repeated scene signature with role progression and reversal. Passage moves from imposed blockage, through voluntary pursuit and regulated noncollision, to moral diversion or erased agency, before ending as compulsory return.
- Canonical scene placements: P1 2A, "Rein, neck, yoke, and raised chin", and 2B, "Blocked horizon"; P2 S2, "Courier from the periphery"; P3 8B, "Solar route and station", and 8C, "Lunar stages and non-collision"; P4 14A, "Preserved instruction on a leveled road", 14B, "Hostile tether and mass diversion", 15A, "Eye and track erased", and 15B, "Recast in place"; P5 19C, "Total custody and return".
- Surprising reach: Straightness is not merely a quality of a path. It is a relation among bodily posture, sight, motive, traffic rules, allegiance, and the authority that fixes an endpoint.
- Synthesis: Every placement preserves course, mover, constraint, and destination, but redistributes control. The courier chooses and the celestial bodies remain regulated; the tethered or erased body loses navigational power; final return closes the progression by making destination certain even when creaturely route-choice has ended.

### 22. Reconstitution from inactivity and dispersal
- Semantic invariant: A still, absent, dispersed, or fragmented substrate is acted upon until organized agency becomes present again.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:12 announces revival, 36:28-32 moves from extinguished generations to total presence, 36:33-34 revives dead ground, 36:51-53 releases and gathers grave-bound bodies, and 36:77-81 argues from first formation and broken bone to renewed embodiment.
- Pericopes and bridge: P1, P2, P3, P4, and P5; repeated causal sequence. Loss of active form is followed by directed intervention, collection or repair, and a renewed output capable of growth, motion, presence, or dispute.
- Canonical scene placements: P1 3A, "Reactivating still life"; P2 6A, "Generations removed from return", and 6B, "Total reassembly into presence"; P3 7A, "Hydraulic cultivation"; P4 11A, "Horn-blast, waking, and grave-exit"; P5 18B, "A drop becomes a manifest disputant", 18C, "Discarded bone repaired into life", and 19A, "Cosmic capacity by measured analogy".
- Surprising reach: Population, soil, fetus-like formation, and bone are not merged into one material class; they share an operation in which lost organization is answered by gathering, growth, repair, or renewed motion.
- Synthesis: The bridge is procedural rather than topical. Each pericope stages a before-state lacking active organized presence, an effective intervention, and an after-state whose agency is visible, so ecological revival and bodily return become mutually clarifying demonstrations of reconstitution.

### 23. Custody becoming obligation and account
- Semantic invariant: Receiving control over a deed, load, resource, dependent being, or person creates an obligation whose outcome exposes whether custody served transfer, use, withholding, or release.
- Surface relation: direct; 36:12 preserves deeds and traces, 36:41 makes descendants entrusted cargo, 36:47 tests provision through expenditure, 36:54 and 36:65 attach settlement and testimony to action, and 36:71-75 tests ownership by service and reciprocal aid.
- Pericopes and bridge: P1, P3, P4, and P5; role progression with contrast and reversal. What begins as preservation in a record becomes carriage and onward provision, then exact account, and finally a test in which owners, dependents, servants, and supposed protectors exchange or lose their claimed roles.
- Canonical scene placements: P1 3B, "Enrolling deeds and traces"; P3 9A, "Loaded vessel and entrusted cargo", and 10B, "Provision that must pass through"; P4 12A, "Exact settlement of action", and 12B, "Sealed mouth, evidentiary limbs"; P5 17A, "Tamed livestock as a service chain", 17B, "Gods that cannot perform reciprocal service", and S15, "Earning release through a written manumission".
- Surprising reach: Custody never remains static possession. Record, cargo, food, livestock, bodily action, and bonded labor each reveal control through what the holder preserves, transfers, yields, withholds, or permits to leave.
- Synthesis: The progression turns possession into responsibility. Entrusted things generate claims on their holders; account makes those claims legible, while failed protection and contractual release reverse the assumption that control is proved simply by retaining another being.
